# DESIGNATED HOUSING PROJECTS — SECTION 68 OF THE ACT

O. Reg. 368/11

### Designated housing projects

1 (1) For the purposes of subsection 68 (1) of the Act, a housing project set out in an item of a Schedule is designated for the transferred housing program referred to in that item.
(2) For the purposes of subsection (1), the housing program referred to in an item is the housing program described in Schedule 1 to Ontario Regulation 367/11 (General) made under the Act for the program category number set out in the item.
2 Omitted (provides for coming into force of provisions of this Regulation).
